# Credits — Wiki Wallpapers 4D

Every wallpaper in this app comes from [Wikimedia Commons](https://commons.wikimedia.org), the free media library behind Wikipedia. **2,322 images by 793 photographers and creators.** Nothing here is re-hosted — images are loaded from Wikimedia's servers and each one is shown with its author, license and source page.

Most images are CC BY / CC BY-SA (attribution required), some are public domain or CC0. If you repost a wallpaper, keep the credit line shown in the app.
